## [2026-09-21 16:43 UTC] Dashboard tick: Move #6.10 attribution-health-alert calculator gains Your-store personalised recovery projection

- **Feature class:** cross-page-intelligence
- **What shipped:** `dashboard/src/components/attribution-health-alert-calculator.tsx` now renders a new **Personalised recovery · tied to Your-store** panel directly above the Path A/B/C/D/E recommendation strip on `/attribution-health-alert-archive`. The panel reads AOV / monthly orders / gross margin from the canonical `ecom-ops:your-store:v1` localStorage key on mount (with cross-tab `storage` event sync), then projects the picked Path's `year1IncrementalAttributionRecovery` band onto the operator's actual revenue base: annual gross revenue · Y1 net recovery (after subtractive infra cost) · recovery as a % of annual revenue · net margin $ + infra payback months. Live-numbers badge lights up when Your-store is set and annualised revenue >= $12k. Falls back gracefully to canonical $75 x 1,000 x 70% defaults with a clear "Industry-median default" badge and CTA to set Your-store on Overview.
- **Why it matters:** the calculator's existing `year1IncrementalAttributionRecoveryLow`/`High` bands are flat dollar figures (Path B = $5k-$15k, Path C = $30k-$200k, Path D = $200k-$1.6M) — they don't scale with the operator's actual revenue. For a $5M-yr brand, a $15k Path B recovery is 0.3% of annual revenue; for a $50M-yr brand the same $15k is 0.03%. The personalised panel re-frames that as `% of annual revenue` + `infra payback months` so the operator can answer the canonical pre-install question "is this alert infra worth it for my size of business?" with their actual numbers.
- **Math:** `annualised_revenue = aov x monthly_orders x 12`; `net_recovery = recovery - cost_mid`; `recovery_pct = net_recovery / annualised_revenue x 100`; `payback_months = cost_mid / (recovery_mid / 12)`; `margin_recovery = net_recovery x gross_margin`. All canonical `PATH_*` constants and `projectPerPathRecovery()` outputs from `lib/attribution-health-alert.ts` are reused — no math drift from the Python CLI (which still passes all 112 tests).
- **Files:** `dashboard/src/components/attribution-health-alert-calculator.tsx` (+213 lines — added `loadYourStore` import + hydration effect + cross-tab `storage` listener + `<PersonalizedRecoveryPanel>` sub-component + insertion into the outputs block).
- **Where to see it:** https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/attribution-health-alert-archive — scroll past the calculator header + 4 input grids; the new personalised panel is the first card in the outputs block. Set Your-store on https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/ to see the "Live numbers" badge light up and the $ projections re-frame against your actual AOV x orders x margin.
- **Deploy status:** **DEPLOY SUCCEEDED.** Live `https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/attribution-health-alert-archive` returns HTTP 200 with the canonical 5-path recommendation strip AND the new personalised recovery panel (verified via curl + grep — `<div id="attribution-health-alert-personalized">` renders with default-fallback message since SSR has no localStorage access; the panel hydrates client-side). Vercel alias rotated in **938ms** from `dashboard-3m62q0893-mattiasadem-5021s-projects.vercel.app` to canonical `ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app`. Next.js build produced 297 static pages, no type errors, no lint warnings.
- **Next action:** natural follow-up — wire the same `Your-store` personalised panel into the 5 remaining calculators that don't yet read `ecom-ops:your-store:v1`: `attribution-quality-audit.tsx` (Gate F cohort roundtrip + Gate G drift both have $/order cost implications), `lifecycle-flow-health-audit.tsx` (revenue-per-1k is exactly the Your-store projection), `generative-ai-engine-calculator.tsx` (Year-1 content-output-value scales with operator margin), `snap-pinterest-attribution-audit.tsx`, and `tiktok-attribution-audit.tsx`. Each follows the same 5-step wiring recipe (load on mount + storage listener + read from `Your-store` + project a personalised $ band + show "Live numbers" badge).

## [2026-09-21 23:55 UTC] Dashboard tick: /playbooks/[slug] detail route ships — 30 playbook pages now click-through (cross-page-intelligence + state-persistence + interactive-tool)

- **What shipped:** A real detail route at `/playbooks/[slug]` that renders each of the 30 playbooks (e.g. `01-abandoned-cart-flow-klaviyo`, `03-checkout-audit-baymard`, `30-quantcast-competitor-creative-monitoring`) as a fully styled page with H1 + freshness + section badges + the Goal lead-block bullets + the full markdown body rendered to HTML (H2/H3 with scroll anchors, bullet/numbered lists, tables, blockquotes, code fences, inline bold/italic/code/links). On the index `/playbooks`, the playbook card title AND the `/playbooks/{file}` slug line are now real `next/link` → click-through to the detail page. Each detail page has a `Mark as shipped` toggle that writes to the same `ecom-ops:shipped-playbooks:v1` localStorage key the index tracker and the `RecentlyShippedPlaybooksCard` on `/` already read — flipping the toggle on the detail page instantly updates the badge on the index AND the progress card on Overview. Notes field surfaces the operator's one-line outcome back on the index tracker.
- **Why this matters:** Before this tick, `/playbooks` was a search-and-filter index with NO click-through — operators could see the Goal bullets + section preview, but to actually read the runbook they had to scroll inside `<details>` or leave the dashboard for the raw markdown. Now each card is one click from a full SSR-rendered playbook page with `force-static` HTML output. The shipped toggle was the #1 missing piece of cross-page intelligence: the index tracker wrote the state, but there was no first-class place to flip it from.
- **What file(s) changed:**
  - `dashboard/app/playbooks/[slug]/page.tsx` (NEW, 11.5kB) — detail route, 30 prerendered paths via `generateStaticParams` over `content.playbooks[]`; reads raw markdown from `src/playbooks/{file}.md` (build-time copy), falls back to `/data/workspace/ecommerce-ops/playbooks/{file}.md` for local dev.
  - `dashboard/src/components/playbook-shipped-toggle.tsx` (NEW, 4.7kB) — client component; `useEffect` hydrate from `loadShippedPlaybooks()` on mount, persist via `saveShippedPlaybooks(shipped)` on change, emit `ecom-ops:shipped-playbooks:update` custom event for same-tab cross-component sync.

Now `parse-content.mjs` writes `src/playbooks/*.md` (30 files) on every run.
  - `dashboard/src/playbooks/*.md` (NEW, 30 files, ~1.4 MB total) — build-time copies of the source playbooks, refreshed every parse run.
  - `dashboard/src/lib/content.json` — refreshed (counts unchanged: 30 playbooks).
